# Commands · E · part 3

1,481 entries, evolve-solution to export. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **evolve-solution**: Oracle-Aware Evolutionary Mode. Turn a fuzzy goal into competing, falsifiable variants judged by an audited oracle — never a single un-tested "solution". Use for: reviving a stalled project with the smallest valuable mutation, finding a non-obvious fix (e.g. lowering a RAG hallucination rate), or choosing among competing architectures by tournament instead of taste. Triggers: /evolve-solution, "evolve a solution", "find the minimal mutation", "tournament of variants", "non-obvious way to". NOT for: a known one-line fix, a factual lookup, or any task with an obvious single answer. ([sergeeey/Claude-cod-top-2026](https://github.com/sergeeey/Claude-cod-top-2026/blob/HEAD/commands/evolve-solution.md) · ★8)
- **evolve-spec**: Evolve an auto-develop capability spec by analyzing results and discovering new capabilities ([aminry/auto-claude-plugins](https://github.com/aminry/auto-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/auto-develop/commands/evolve-spec.md) · ★1)
- **evolve-split**: **Command:** `/evolve:split {component} [depth]` **Version:** 1.0.0 **Purpose:** Analyze a domain or component and suggest how to split it into smaller, more focused pieces to reduce complexity and improve maintainability. ([nathanvale/vtm-cli](https://github.com/nathanvale/vtm-cli/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/evolve-split.md))

- **evolve-system**: Evolve an existing system architecture based on new requirements ([CrazyMeal/claude-architect-marketplace](https://github.com/CrazyMeal/claude-architect-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/architecture-design/commands/evolve-system.md) · ★1)
- **evolve-to-plugin**: **Command:** `/evolve:to-plugin {domain} [version] [description]` **Version:** 1.0.0 **Purpose:** Package an entire domain (commands + skills + MCPs + hooks) into a complete, shareable plugin that team members can install. ([nathanvale/vtm-cli](https://github.com/nathanvale/vtm-cli/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/evolve-to-plugin.md))
